# Graph Report - gestion-clinique  (2026-11-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3409 files · ~2,845,290 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 242 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 116, .example 42, .lock 31)

## Summary
- 53053 nodes · 129028 edges · 1642 communities (1254 shown, 388 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 92% EXTRACTED · 8% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9910 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f97f8ce0`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
